where the last equation is from the fact that
\hat{y}_{0}=\tilde{y}_{0}=x_{0}
and
\hat{v}_{E}=\tilde{v}_{E}=1
.

Thus, with Assumption
1
, it holds that:

\displaystyle\sum_{e=1}^{E}\sum_{t=1}^{T+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{u}^{(t)}_{e}-\nabla_{e}\ell(\boldsymbol{w}^{(t)})\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 32L_{\nabla\ell}^{2}\left(\dfrac{p+m}{m^{2}(1-(1-p)(1-\frac{m}{2}))}+\dfrac{1}{m^{2}}\right)\sum_{e=1}^{E}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|w^{(t+1)}_{e}-w^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+8(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}_{e}^{(t)}-\hat{v}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]+8(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2}\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t)}-\hat{y}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+4T\sigma^{2}\dfrac{(2-p)m-(1-p)m^{2}}{1-(1-p)(1-m)^{2}}+\dfrac{3}{m}\sum_{e=1}^{E}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{u}^{(1)}_{e}-\nabla_{e}\ell(\boldsymbol{w}^{(1)})\right\|^{2}\right].

Thus, we finish the proof of this lemma.
∎

A.2

Compress error analysis

Here we consider the compress error of forward and backward propagation as the following lemma:

Lemma 4

(Compress error of forward and backward propagation)
.

Suppose Assumption
3
holds, then for
e=1,2,\cdots,E-1
and
T_{1}>T_{0}\geq 1
we have:

\displaystyle\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T_{1}+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}^{(t)}_{e}-v^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\dfrac{\omega_{B}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2}}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|v^{(t+1)}_{e}-v^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right]+\dfrac{\omega_{B}}{1-\omega_{B}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}^{(T_{0})}_{e}-v^{(T_{0})}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right],

(37a)

\displaystyle\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T_{1}+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}^{(t)}_{e}-{{y}}^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\dfrac{\omega_{F}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{y}}^{(t+1)}_{e}-{{y}}^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right]+\dfrac{\omega_{F}}{1-\omega_{F}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}^{(T_{0})}_{e}-{{y}}^{(T_{0})}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right].

(37b)

Proof.

Firstly we consider the term
\left\|\tilde{v}^{(t)}_{e}-{{v}}^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}
for
e=1,2,\cdots,E-1
. According to Algorithm
3
, we have:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}^{(t)}_{e}-{{v}}^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\middle|\mathcal{G}_{e}^{(t)}\right]=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}^{(t-1)}_{e}+\mathcal{C}({{v}}^{(t)}_{e}-\tilde{v}^{(t-1)}_{e})-{{v}}^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\middle|\mathcal{G}_{e}^{(t)}\right]\leq\omega_{B}^{2}\left\|\tilde{v}^{(t-1)}_{e}-{{v}}^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}

(38)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\omega_{B}\left\|\tilde{v}^{(t-1)}_{e}-{{v}}^{(t-1)}_{e}\right\|^{2}+\dfrac{\omega_{B}^{2}}{1-\omega_{B}}\left\|{{v}}^{(t)}_{e}-{{v}}^{(t-1)}_{e}\right\|^{2},

where the first inequality is due to Assumption
3
and the second inequality uses Young’s inequality. Then, taking expectation on both sides and then taking summation over
t=T_{0}+1,\cdots,T_{1}+1
, we have:

\displaystyle\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T_{1}+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}^{(t)}_{e}-{{v}}^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right]\leq\omega_{B}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}^{(t)}_{e}-{{v}}^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right]+\dfrac{\omega_{B}^{2}}{1-\omega_{B}}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{v}}^{(t+1)}_{e}-{{v}}^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right].

(39)

Then, we can get:

\displaystyle\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T_{1}+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}^{(t)}_{e}-{{v}}^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right]\leq\dfrac{\omega_{B}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2}}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{v}}^{(t+1)}_{e}-{{v}}^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right]+\dfrac{\omega_{B}}{1-\omega_{B}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}^{(T_{0})}_{e}-{{v}}^{(T_{0})}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right].

Thus, Eq. (
37a
) holds.

Next, we consider the term
\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}^{(t)}_{e}-{{y}}^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}
for
e=1,2,\cdots,E-1
. According to Algorithm
2
, we have:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}^{(t)}_{e}-{{y}}^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\middle|\mathcal{F}_{e}^{(t)}\right]=

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}^{(t-1)}_{e}+\mathcal{C}({{y}}^{(t)}_{e}-\tilde{{{y}}}^{(t-1)}_{e})-{{y}}^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\middle|\mathcal{F}_{e}^{(t)}\right]\leq\omega_{F}^{2}\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}^{(t-1)}_{e}-{{y}}^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}

(40)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\omega_{F}\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}^{(t-1)}_{e}-{{y}}^{(t-1)}_{e}\right\|^{2}+\dfrac{\omega_{F}^{2}}{1-\omega_{F}}\left\|{{y}}^{(t)}_{e}-{{y}}^{(t-1)}_{e}\right\|^{2},

where the first inequality is due to Assumption
3
and the second inequality uses Young’s inequality. Then, taking expectation on both sides and then taking summation over
t=T_{0}+1,\cdots,T_{1}+1
, we have:

\displaystyle\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T_{1}+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}^{(t)}_{e}-{{y}}^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right]\leq\omega_{F}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}^{(t)}_{e}-{{y}}^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right]+\dfrac{\omega_{F}^{2}}{1-\omega_{F}}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{y}}^{(t+1)}_{e}-{{y}}^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right].

(41)

Then, we can get:

\displaystyle\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T_{1}+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}^{(t)}_{e}-{{y}}^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right]\leq\dfrac{\omega_{F}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{y}}^{(t+1)}_{e}-{{y}}^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right]+\dfrac{\omega_{F}}{1-\omega_{F}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}^{(T_{0})}_{e}-{{y}}^{(T_{0})}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right].

Thus, Eq. (
37b
) holds.
∎

A.3

Error accumulation in forward propagation

With Lemma
4
, we can present the following lemma to show the analysis of the error term
\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t)}-\hat{y}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}
. Then we can obtain the error accumulation in forward propagation.

Lemma 5
.

Suppose Assumption
1
and
3
holds, then for any
T_{1}>T_{0}\geq 1
we have:

\displaystyle\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T_{1}+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t)}-\hat{y}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}\sum_{\iota=e}^{E-1}2(2L_{a}^{2})^{\iota-e}\dfrac{\omega_{F}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{y}}^{(t+1)}_{e}-{{y}}^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right]

(42)

\displaystyle+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}\sum_{\iota=e}^{E-1}2(2L_{a}^{2})^{\iota-e}\dfrac{\omega_{F}}{1-\omega_{F}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}^{(T_{0})}_{e}-{{y}}^{(T_{0})}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right].

Proof.

For
1\leq e\leq E-1
, we have:

\displaystyle\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t)}-\hat{y}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\leq

\displaystyle 2\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t)}-{{y}}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}+2\left\|{{y}}_{e}^{(t)}-\hat{y}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}

(43)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t)}-{{y}}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}+2\left\|a_{e}(\tilde{{{y}}}_{e-1}^{(t)},w_{e}^{(t)})-a_{e}(\hat{y}_{e-1}^{(t)},w_{e}^{(t)})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t)}-{{y}}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}+2L_{a}^{2}\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{e-1}^{(t)}-\hat{y}_{e-1}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\leq\cdots\leq\sum_{\iota=1}^{e}2(2L_{a}^{2})^{e-\iota}\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{\iota}^{(t)}-{{y}}_{\iota}^{(t)}\right\|^{2},

where the second inequality is due to Assumption
1
.

Taking expectation and then taking summation on both sides of (
43
) over
t=T_{0}+1,\cdots,T_{1}+1
, then we have:

\displaystyle\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T_{1}+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t)}-\hat{y}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]\leq\sum_{\iota=1}^{e}2(2L_{a}^{2})^{e-\iota}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T_{1}+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{\iota}^{(t)}-{{y}}_{\iota}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

(44)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{\iota=1}^{e}2(2L_{a}^{2})^{e-\iota}\dfrac{\omega_{F}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{y}}^{(t+1)}_{\iota}-{{y}}^{(t)}_{\iota}\right\|^{2}\right]+\sum_{\iota=1}^{e}2(2L_{a}^{2})^{e-\iota}\dfrac{\omega_{F}}{1-\omega_{F}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}^{(T_{0})}_{\iota}-{{y}}^{(T_{0})}_{\iota}\right\|^{2}\right],

where the last inequality is due to the result of (
37b
).

Taking summation on both sides of (
44
) over
e
, we can get:

\displaystyle\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T_{1}+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t)}-\hat{y}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}\sum_{\iota=e}^{E-1}2(2L_{a}^{2})^{\iota-e}\dfrac{\omega_{F}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{y}}^{(t+1)}_{e}-{{y}}^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}\sum_{\iota=e}^{E-1}2(2L_{a}^{2})^{\iota-e}\dfrac{\omega_{F}}{1-\omega_{F}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}^{(T_{0})}_{e}-{{y}}^{(T_{0})}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right].

∎

Then, the following lemma shows the analysis of the term
\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t)}-\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t-1)}\right\|^{2}
.

Lemma 6
.

Suppose Assumption
3
and Eq. (
37b
) holds, then for any
T_{1}>T_{0}\geq 1
we have:

\displaystyle\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t+1)}-\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]\leq\dfrac{8}{(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{y}}_{e}^{(t+1)}-{{y}}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]+\dfrac{8}{1-\omega_{F}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(T_{0})}-{{y}}_{e}^{(T_{0})}\right\|^{2}\right].

(45)

Proof.

For
t=T_{0}+1,\cdots,T_{1}+1
, we have:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t)}-\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t-1)}\right\|^{2}\middle|\mathcal{F}^{(t)}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathcal{C}\left({{y}}_{e}^{(t)}-\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t-1)}\right)\right\|^{2}\middle|\mathcal{F}^{(t)}\right]

(46)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1+\dfrac{1}{\omega_{F}}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\mathcal{C}\left({{y}}_{e}^{(t)}-\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t-1)}\right)-\left({{y}}_{e}^{(t)}-\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t-1)}\right)\right\|^{2}\middle|\mathcal{F}^{(t)}\right]+(1+\omega_{F})\left\|{{y}}_{e}^{(t)}-\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t-1)}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1+\omega_{F})^{2}\left\|{{y}}_{e}^{(t)}-\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t-1)}\right\|^{2}\leq 8\left\|{{y}}_{e}^{(t)}-{{y}}_{e}^{(t-1)}\right\|^{2}+8\left\|{{y}}_{e}^{(t-1)}-\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t-1)}\right\|^{2},

where the first inequality is due to Young’s inequality, the second inequality is due to Assumption
3
, and the last inequality is due to
\omega_{F}\in[0,1)
in Assumption
3
.

Then, taking expectation and taking summation on both sides of (
46
) over
t=T_{0}+1,\cdots,T_{1}+1
, we have:

\displaystyle\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t+1)}-\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]\leq

\displaystyle 8\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{y}}_{e}^{(t+1)}-{{y}}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]+8\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{y}}_{e}^{(t)}-\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\dfrac{8}{(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{y}}_{e}^{(t+1)}-{{y}}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]+\dfrac{8}{1-\omega_{F}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(T_{0})}-{{y}}_{e}^{(T_{0})}\right\|^{2}\right],

where the last inequality is due to Eq. (
37b
) and the fact that
1+\dfrac{\omega_{F}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}\leq\dfrac{1}{(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}
as
\omega_{F}\in[0,1)
.
∎

A.4

Convergence rate of the case
E=2

We firstly consider the proof of the case
E=2
for
Clapping
-FU . In that case, there is once communication in both forward and backward propagation. Nevertheless, the analysis of the error accumulation and propagation in backward is not complex in this case. Thus, the proof of that case is more simple than the general case but can show how error feedback and lazy sampling benefit the convergence.

Lemma 7

(Convergence rate of
Clapping
-
FU
in the case
E=2
)
.

Suppose Assumption
1
,
2
, and
3
hold. Then for
Clapping
-
FU
, there exist
\gamma,m>0
such that:

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla\ell(\boldsymbol{w}^{(t)})\right\|^{2}\right]\lesssim\dfrac{\sigma}{\sqrt{T}}+\dfrac{1}{T(1-\omega_{B})(1-\omega_{F})}.

(47)

Proof.

Substituting
E=2
into (
13
), we have:

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T+1}\sum_{e=1}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{u}^{(t)}_{e}-\nabla_{e}\ell(\boldsymbol{w}^{(t)})\right\|^{2}\right]

(48)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 32L_{\nabla\ell}^{2}\left(\dfrac{p+m}{m^{2}(1-(1-p)(1-\frac{m}{2}))}+\dfrac{1}{m^{2}}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{e=1}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|w^{(t+1)}_{e}-w^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+8(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}\sum_{t=2}^{T+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}_{1}^{(t)}-\hat{v}_{1}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]+8(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2}\sum_{t=2}^{T+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{1}^{(t)}-\hat{y}_{1}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+4T\sigma^{2}\dfrac{(2-p)m-(1-p)m^{2}}{1-(1-p)(1-m)^{2}}+\dfrac{3}{m}\sum_{e=1}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{u}^{(1)}_{e}-\nabla_{e}\ell(\boldsymbol{w}^{(1)})\right\|^{2}\right].

For the first
T-1
iterations, suppose
1=Q_{1}<Q_{2}<\cdots<Q_{r_{0}}\leq T
are the all moments at which the sample is randomly obtained from
\mathcal{D}
. We also denote
Q_{r_{0}+1}=T+1
.

For any
1\leq r\leq r_{0}
, we note that
\tilde{{{y}}}^{(Q_{r})}_{1}={{y}}^{(Q_{r})}_{1}=\hat{{{y}}}^{(Q_{r})}_{1}
as we do not compress the activation and gradients in
Clapping
-
FU
. Thus, substituting
E=2
into (
42
), we can get:

\displaystyle\sum_{t=Q_{r}+1}^{Q_{r+1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{1}^{(t)}-\hat{y}_{1}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{t=Q_{r}+1}^{Q_{r+1}-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{1}^{(t)}-\hat{y}_{1}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]\leq C_{y}^{2}\dfrac{\omega_{F}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}\sum_{t=Q_{r}}^{Q_{r+1}-2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{y}}^{(t+1)}_{1}-{{y}}^{(t)}_{1}\right\|^{2}\right].

(49)

Taking summation over
r=1,2,\cdots,r_{0}
, it holds that:

\displaystyle\sum_{t=2}^{T+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{1}^{(t)}-\hat{y}_{1}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\sum_{r=1}^{r_{0}}\sum_{t=Q_{r}+1}^{Q_{r+1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{1}^{(t)}-\hat{y}_{1}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

(50)

\displaystyle\leq C_{y}^{2}\dfrac{\omega_{F}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}\sum_{r=1}^{r_{0}}\sum_{t=Q_{r}}^{Q_{r+1}-2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{y}}^{(t+1)}_{1}-{{y}}^{(t)}_{1}\right\|^{2}\right].

Then, use the fact that
\nabla a_{2}
is Lipschitz continous, we have:

\displaystyle\left\|{{v}}^{(t)}_{1}-\hat{v}^{(t)}_{1}\right\|^{2}=\left\|\nabla a_{2}(\tilde{{{y}}}^{(t)}_{1},w_{2}^{(t)})-\nabla a_{2}(\hat{y}^{(t)}_{1},w_{2}^{(t)})\right\|^{2}\leq L_{\nabla a}^{2}\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}^{(t)}_{1}-\hat{y}^{(t)}_{1}\right\|^{2}.

(51)

From
\tilde{v}^{(Q_{r})}_{1}={{v}}^{(Q_{r})}_{1}=\hat{v}^{(Q_{r})}_{1}
, we can obtain that:

\displaystyle\sum_{t=Q_{r}+1}^{Q_{r+1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}_{1}^{(t)}-\hat{v}_{1}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]\leq 2\sum_{t=Q_{r}+1}^{Q_{r+1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}_{1}^{(t)}-{{{v}}}_{1}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]+2\sum_{t=Q_{r}+1}^{Q_{r+1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{v}}_{1}^{(t)}-\hat{v}_{1}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

(52)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\sum_{t=Q_{r}+1}^{Q_{r+1}-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}_{1}^{(t)}-{{{v}}}_{1}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]+2\sum_{t=Q_{r}+1}^{Q_{r+1}-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{v}}_{1}^{(t)}-\hat{v}_{1}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\dfrac{\omega_{B}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2}}\sum_{t=Q_{r}}^{Q_{r+1}-2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{v}}^{(t+1)}_{1}-{{v}}^{(t)}_{1}\right\|^{2}\right]+2L_{\nabla a}^{2}\sum_{t=Q_{r}+1}^{Q_{r+1}-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}^{(t)}_{1}-\hat{y}^{(t)}_{1}\right\|^{2}\right],

where the second inequality is due to (
37a
) and (
51
).
Taking summation over
r
, it holds that:

\displaystyle\sum_{t=2}^{T+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}_{1}^{(t)}-\hat{v}_{1}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

(53)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\dfrac{\omega_{B}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2}}\sum_{r=1}^{r_{0}}\sum_{t=Q_{r}}^{Q_{r+1}-2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{v}}^{(t+1)}_{1}-{{v}}^{(t)}_{1}\right\|^{2}\right]+2L_{\nabla a}^{2}\sum_{t=2}^{T+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}^{(t)}_{1}-\hat{y}^{(t)}_{1}\right\|^{2}\right],

Plugging Eq. (
50
) and Eq. (
53
) into Eq. (
48
), it holds that:

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T+1}\sum_{e=1}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{u}^{(t)}_{e}-\nabla_{e}\ell(\boldsymbol{w}^{(t)})\right\|^{2}\right]

(54)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 32L_{\nabla\ell}^{2}\left(\dfrac{p+m}{m^{2}(1-(1-p)(1-\frac{m}{2}))}+\dfrac{1}{m^{2}}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{e=1}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|w^{(t+1)}_{e}-w^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+16(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}\dfrac{\omega_{B}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2}}\sum_{r=1}^{r_{0}}\sum_{t=Q_{r}}^{Q_{r+1}-2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{v}}^{(t+1)}_{1}-{{v}}^{(t)}_{1}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\left(8(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2}+16(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}L_{\nabla a}^{2}\right)C_{y}^{2}\dfrac{\omega_{F}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}\sum_{r=1}^{r_{0}}\sum_{t=Q_{r}}^{Q_{r+1}-2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{y}}^{(t+1)}_{1}-{{y}}^{(t)}_{1}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+4T\sigma^{2}\dfrac{(2-p)m-(1-p)m^{2}}{1-(1-p)(1-m)^{2}}+\dfrac{3}{m}\sum_{e=1}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{u}^{(1)}_{e}-\nabla_{e}\ell(\boldsymbol{w}^{(1)})\right\|^{2}\right].

Note that
E=2
, thus for any
1\leq r\leq r_{0}
we have:

\displaystyle\sum_{t=Q_{r}}^{Q_{r+1}-2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{v}}^{(t+1)}_{1}-{{v}}^{(t)}_{1}\right\|^{2}\right]=\sum_{t=Q_{r}}^{Q_{r+1}-2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla_{1}a_{2}(\tilde{{{y}}}^{(t+1)}_{1},w_{2}^{(t+1)})-\nabla_{1}a_{2}(\tilde{{{y}}}^{(t)}_{1},w_{2}^{(t)})\right\|^{2}\right]

(55)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle L_{\nabla a}^{2}\sum_{t=Q_{r}}^{Q_{r+1}-2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}^{(t+1)}_{1}-\tilde{{{y}}}^{(t)}_{1}\right\|^{2}+\left\|w_{2}^{(t+1)}-w_{2}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle L_{\nabla a}^{2}\dfrac{8}{(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}\sum_{t=Q_{r}}^{Q_{r+1}-2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{y}}_{1}^{(t+1)}-{{y}}_{1}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]+L_{\nabla a}^{2}\sum_{t=Q_{r}}^{Q_{r+1}-2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|w_{2}^{(t+1)}-w_{2}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right],

where the first inequality is due to Assumption
1
and the second inequality is due to (
45
).

Taking summation over
r
, it holds that:

\displaystyle\sum_{r=1}^{r_{0}}\sum_{t=Q_{r}}^{Q_{r+1}-2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{v}}^{(t+1)}_{1}-{{v}}^{(t)}_{1}\right\|^{2}\right]

(56)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle L_{\nabla a}^{2}\dfrac{8}{(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}\sum_{r=1}^{r_{0}}\sum_{t=Q_{r}}^{Q_{r+1}-2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{y}}_{1}^{(t+1)}-{{y}}_{1}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]+L_{\nabla a}^{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|w_{2}^{(t+1)}-w_{2}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right],

Then as
x^{(t+1)}=x^{(t)}
for any
t=Q_{r},\cdots,Q_{r+1}-2
, we also have

\displaystyle\sum_{t=Q_{r}}^{Q_{r+1}-2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{y}}^{(t+1)}_{1}-{{y}}^{(t)}_{1}\right\|^{2}\right]=\sum_{t=Q_{r}}^{Q_{r+1}-2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{y}^{(t+1)}_{1}-\hat{y}^{(t)}_{1}\right\|^{2}\right]

(57)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{t=Q_{r}}^{Q_{r+1}-2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|a_{1}(x^{(t+1)},w_{1}^{(t+1)})-a_{1}(x^{(t)},w_{1}^{(t)})\right\|^{2}\right]\leq L_{a}^{2}\sum_{t=Q_{r}}^{Q_{r+1}-2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|w_{1}^{(t+1)}-w_{1}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right],

where the inequality is due to Assumption
1
.

Taking summation over
r
, it holds that:

\displaystyle\sum_{r=1}^{r_{0}}\sum_{t=Q_{r}}^{Q_{r+1}-2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{y}}^{(t+1)}_{1}-{{y}}^{(t)}_{1}\right\|^{2}\right]\leq L_{a}^{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|w^{(t+1)}-w^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right],

(58)

Plugging (
56
) and (
58
) into (
54
), we can get:

\displaystyle\sum_{t=1}^{T+1}\sum_{e=1}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{u}^{(t)}_{e}-\nabla_{e}\ell(\boldsymbol{w}^{(t)})\right\|^{2}\right]

(59)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 32L_{\nabla\ell}^{2}\left(\dfrac{p+m}{m^{2}(1-(1-p)(1-\frac{m}{2}))}+\dfrac{1}{m^{2}}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{e=1}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|w^{(t+1)}_{e}-w^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+16L_{\nabla_{a}}^{2}(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}\dfrac{\omega_{B}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2}}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|w^{(t+1)}_{2}-w^{(t)}_{2}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\left(\left(8(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2}+16(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}L_{\nabla a}^{2}\right)C_{y}^{2}\dfrac{\omega_{F}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}+128L_{\nabla_{a}}^{2}(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}\dfrac{\omega_{B}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2}(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\cdot L_{a}^{2}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|w^{(t+1)}-w^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+4T\sigma^{2}\dfrac{(2-p)m-(1-p)m^{2}}{1-(1-p)(1-m)^{2}}+\dfrac{3}{m}\sum_{e=1}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{u}^{(1)}_{e}-\nabla_{e}\ell(\boldsymbol{w}^{(1)})\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{w}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\boldsymbol{w}^{(t+1)}-\boldsymbol{w}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]+4T\sigma^{2}\dfrac{(2-p)m-(1-p)m^{2}}{1-(1-p)(1-m)^{2}}+\dfrac{3}{m}\sum_{e=1}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{u}^{(1)}_{e}-\nabla_{e}\ell(\boldsymbol{w}^{(1)})\right\|^{2}\right].

where:

\displaystyle C_{w}=

\displaystyle 32L_{\nabla\ell}^{2}\left(\dfrac{p+m}{m^{2}(1-(1-p)(1-\frac{m}{2}))}+\dfrac{1}{m^{2}}\right)+16L_{\nabla_{a}}^{2}(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}\dfrac{\omega_{B}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2}}

\displaystyle+\left(8(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2}+16(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}L_{\nabla a}^{2}\right)L_{a}^{2}C_{y}^{2}\dfrac{\omega_{F}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}+128L_{\nabla_{a}}^{2}(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}L_{a}^{2}\dfrac{\omega_{B}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2}(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}.

Plugging
E=2
into (
8
), combining it with (
59
), and then taken
p=m
, we can get

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla\ell(\boldsymbol{w}^{(t)})\right\|^{2}\right]

(60)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\dfrac{2}{\gamma T}\mathbb{E}\left[\ell(\boldsymbol{w}^{(1)})-\inf_{\boldsymbol{w}}\ell(\boldsymbol{w})\right]+\dfrac{1}{T}\left(C_{w}-\dfrac{1}{2\gamma^{2}}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\boldsymbol{w}^{(t+1)}-\boldsymbol{w}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+4\sigma^{2}\dfrac{(2-p)m-(1-p)m^{2}}{1-(1-p)(1-m)^{2}}+\dfrac{3}{mT}\sum_{e=1}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{u}^{(1)}_{e}-\nabla_{e}\ell(\boldsymbol{w}^{(1)})\right\|^{2}\right].

Let
p=p_{0}
as a constant with the order of
\mathcal{O}(1)
, and let:

\displaystyle m

\displaystyle\sim\left(\dfrac{1}{(1-\omega_{B})(1-\omega_{F})}+\sigma\sqrt{T}\right)^{-1},\quad\gamma\sim\left(\dfrac{1}{(1-\omega_{B})(1-\omega_{F})}+\sigma\sqrt{T}\right)^{-1}\ \text{and}\ m,\gamma\leq 1.

Then, as
\gamma
has the same order as
m
with respect to
\omega_{B},\omega_{F},\sigma,T
, it holds that
C_{w}-\dfrac{1}{2\gamma^{2}}\leq 0
if
\gamma/m
is sufficiently small.

Thus, we have:

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla\ell(\boldsymbol{w}^{(t)})\right\|^{2}\right]\lesssim

\displaystyle\dfrac{\sigma}{\sqrt{T}}+\dfrac{1}{T(1-\omega_{B})(1-\omega_{F})}.

∎

A.5

Error accumulation in backward propagation

Here, we analysis the error accumulation and propagation in backward propagation. In the beginning, we present a lemma that shows the error analysis of
\left\|\tilde{v}^{(t)}_{e}-\hat{v}^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}
.

Lemma 8
.

Suppose Assumption
1
and
3
holds, then for any
T_{1}>T_{0}\geq 1
and
e=1,2,\cdots,E-1
we have:

\displaystyle\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T_{1}+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}^{(t)}_{e}-\hat{v}^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right]\leq 2\dfrac{\omega_{B}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2}}\sum_{\iota=e}^{E-1}(2(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2})^{\iota-e}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{v}}^{(t+1)}_{\iota}-{{v}}^{(t)}_{\iota}\right\|^{2}\right]

(61)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle(2(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2})\sum_{\iota=e}^{E-1}(2(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2})^{\iota-e}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T_{1}+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{\iota}^{(t)}-\hat{y}_{\iota}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]+2\dfrac{\omega_{B}}{1-\omega_{B}}\sum_{\iota=e}^{E-1}(2(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2})^{\iota-e}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}^{(T_{0})}_{\iota}-{{v}}^{(T_{0})}_{\iota}\right\|^{2}\right].

Proof.

For
1\leq e\leq E-1
, we have:

\displaystyle\left\|\tilde{v}^{(t)}_{e}-\hat{v}^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\leq

\displaystyle 2\left\|\tilde{v}^{(t)}_{e}-{{v}}^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}+2\left\|{{v}}^{(t)}_{e}-\hat{v}^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}

(62)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\left\|\tilde{v}^{(t)}_{e}-{{v}}^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}+2\left\|\nabla_{1}a_{e+1}(\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t)},w_{e+1}^{(t)})^{{}^{\mathsf{T}}}\tilde{v}_{e+1}^{(t)}-\nabla_{1}a_{e+1}(\hat{y}_{e}^{(t)},w_{e+1}^{(t)})^{{}^{\mathsf{T}}}\hat{v}_{e+1}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\left\|\tilde{v}^{(t)}_{e}-{{v}}^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}+2(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2}\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t)}-\hat{y}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}+2(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}\left\|\tilde{v}_{e+1}^{(t)}-\hat{v}_{e+1}^{(t)}\right\|^{2},

where the second inequality is due to (
12
). Then, we can get:

\displaystyle\left\|\tilde{v}^{(t)}_{e}-\hat{v}^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\leq\sum_{\iota=e}^{E-1}2(2(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2})^{\iota-e}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}^{(t)}_{\iota}-{{v}}^{(t)}_{\iota}\right\|^{2}+(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2}\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{\iota}^{(t)}-\hat{y}_{\iota}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

(63)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\sum_{\iota=e}^{E-1}(2(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2})^{\iota-e}\left\|\tilde{v}^{(t)}_{\iota}-{{v}}^{(t)}_{\iota}\right\|^{2}+(2(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2})\sum_{\iota=e}^{E-1}(2(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2})^{\iota-e}\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{\iota}^{(t)}-\hat{y}_{\iota}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}.

Taking expectation and then taking summation on both sides of (
63
) over
t=T_{0}+1,\cdots,T_{1}+1
, then we gan get

\displaystyle\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T_{1}+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}^{(t)}_{e}-\hat{v}^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right]

(64)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\sum_{\iota=e}^{E-1}(2(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2})^{\iota-e}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T_{1}+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}^{(t)}_{\iota}-{{v}}^{(t)}_{\iota}\right\|^{2}\right]+(2(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2})\sum_{\iota=e}^{E-1}(2(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2})^{\iota-e}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T_{1}+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{\iota}^{(t)}-\hat{y}_{\iota}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\dfrac{\omega_{B}^{2}}{(1\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt\omega_{B})^{2}}\hskip-1.42262pt\sum_{\iota=e}^{E-1}(2(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2})^{\iota-e}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{v}}^{(t+1)}_{\iota}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt{{v}}^{(t)}_{\iota}\right\|^{2}\right]\hskip-1.42262pt+\hskip-1.42262pt2\dfrac{\omega_{B}}{1-\omega_{B}}\sum_{\iota=e}^{E-1}(2(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2})^{\iota-e}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}^{(T_{0})}_{\iota}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt{{v}}^{(T_{0})}_{\iota}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+(2(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2})\sum_{\iota=e}^{E-1}(2(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2})^{\iota-e}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T_{1}+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{\iota}^{(t)}-\hat{y}_{\iota}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right],

where the last inequality is due to Eq. (
37a
).
∎

Eq. (
61
) suggest that we can analysis the error term
\left\|{{v}}_{e}^{(t+1)}-{{v}}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}
. Thus, we consider the following lemma:

Lemma 9
.

Suppose Assumption
1
and (
45
) hold, then for any
T_{1}>T_{0}\geq 1
and
e=1,2,\cdots,E-1
we have:

\displaystyle\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{v}}^{(t+1)}_{e}-{{v}}^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right]\leq 5(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T_{1}+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}_{e+1}^{(t)}-\hat{v}_{e+1}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]+5(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{v}_{e+1}^{(t+1)}-\hat{v}_{e+1}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

(65)

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle 5(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|w_{e+1}^{(t+1)}-w_{e+1}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]+\dfrac{40(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2}}{(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{y}}_{e}^{(t+1)}-{{y}}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\dfrac{40(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2}}{1-\omega_{F}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(T_{0})}-{{y}}_{e}^{(T_{0})}\right\|^{2}\right]+\dfrac{5}{2}(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}_{e+1}^{(T_{0})}-\hat{v}_{e+1}^{(T_{0})}\right\|^{2}\right].

Proof.

Firstly, we consider the case of
e=1,\cdots,E-1
, we have:

\displaystyle\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{v}}^{(t+1)}_{e}-{{v}}^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right]

(66)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla_{1}a_{e+1}(\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t+1)},w_{e+1}^{(t+1)})^{{}^{\mathsf{T}}}\tilde{v}_{e+1}^{(t+1)}-\nabla_{1}a_{e+1}(\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t)},w_{e+1}^{(t)})^{{}^{\mathsf{T}}}\tilde{v}_{e+1}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\dfrac{5}{2}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla_{1}a_{e+1}(\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t+1)},w_{e+1}^{(t+1)})^{{}^{\mathsf{T}}}\tilde{v}_{e+1}^{(t+1)}-\nabla_{1}a_{e+1}(\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t+1)},w_{e+1}^{(t+1)})^{{}^{\mathsf{T}}}\hat{v}_{e+1}^{(t+1)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\dfrac{5}{2}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla_{1}a_{e+1}(\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t)},w_{e+1}^{(t)})^{{}^{\mathsf{T}}}\tilde{v}_{e+1}^{(t)}-\nabla_{1}a_{e+1}(\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t)},w_{e+1}^{(t)})^{{}^{\mathsf{T}}}\hat{v}_{e+1}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+5\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla_{1}a_{e+1}(\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t+1)},w_{e+1}^{(t+1)})^{{}^{\mathsf{T}}}\hat{v}_{e+1}^{(t+1)}-\nabla_{1}a_{e+1}(\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t)},w_{e+1}^{(t)})^{{}^{\mathsf{T}}}\hat{v}_{e+1}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right].

Then, we have:

\displaystyle\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{v}}^{(t+1)}_{e}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt{{v}}^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right]

(67)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\dfrac{5}{2}(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}_{e+1}^{(t+1)}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt\hat{v}_{e+1}^{(t+1)}\right\|^{2}\right]\hskip-1.42262pt+\hskip-1.42262pt\dfrac{5}{2}(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}_{e+1}^{(t)}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt\hat{v}_{e+1}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\hskip-1.42262pt5(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{v}_{e+1}^{(t+1)}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt\hat{v}_{e+1}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]\hskip-1.42262pt+\hskip-1.42262pt5(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t+1)}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]\hskip-1.42262pt+\hskip-1.42262pt\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|w_{e+1}^{(t+1)}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262ptw_{e+1}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 5(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T_{1}+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}_{e+1}^{(t)}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt\hat{v}_{e+1}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]\hskip-1.42262pt+\hskip-1.42262pt5(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{v}_{e+1}^{(t+1)}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt\hat{v}_{e+1}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\hskip-1.42262pt5(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|w_{e+1}^{(t+1)}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262ptw_{e+1}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]\hskip-1.42262pt+\hskip-1.42262pt\dfrac{40(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2}}{(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{y}}_{e}^{(t+1)}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt{{y}}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\hskip-1.42262pt\dfrac{40(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2}}{1-\omega_{F}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(T_{0})}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt{{y}}_{e}^{(T_{0})}\right\|^{2}\right]\hskip-1.42262pt+\hskip-1.42262pt\dfrac{5}{2}(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}_{e+1}^{(T_{0})}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt\hat{v}_{e+1}^{(T_{0})}\right\|^{2}\right],

where the first inequality is due to Eq. (
12
) and the last inequality is due to Eq. (
45
).
∎

The following lemma combines the result of Lemma
8
and Lemma
9
together and give a further analysis of
\left\|\tilde{v}^{(t)}_{e}-\hat{v}^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}
.

Lemma 10
.

Suppose Lemma
8
and Lemma
9
are all satisfied, then there exist coefficients
C_{y,e}^{\circ},C_{W,e}^{\circ},C_{v,e}^{\circ},C_{v,e}^{1},C_{v,e}^{2},C_{\theta,e}^{\circ},C_{\theta,e}^{1}\geq 0
(which has been defined by Eq. (
72
)) for each
e=1,2,\cdots,E-1
and
T_{1}>T_{0}\geq 1
such that:

\displaystyle\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T_{1}+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}^{(t)}_{e}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt\hat{v}^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right]

(68)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}C_{y,e}^{\circ}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T_{1}+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t)}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt\hat{y}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]+\sum_{e=1}^{E}C_{W,e}^{\circ}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|w_{e}^{(t+1)}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262ptw_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}C_{v,e}^{\circ}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{v}_{e}^{(t+1)}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt\hat{v}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}C_{\theta,e}^{\circ}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{y}}_{e}^{(t+1)}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt{{y}}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}C_{\theta,e}^{1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(T_{0})}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt{{y}}_{e}^{(T_{0})}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}C_{v,e}^{1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}_{e}^{(T_{0})}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt\hat{v}_{e}^{(T_{0})}\right\|^{2}\right]+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}C_{v,e}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}_{e}^{(T_{0})}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt{{v}}_{e}^{(T_{0})}\right\|^{2}\right].

Proof.

Combing (
61
) and (
65
) together, we have:

\displaystyle\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T_{1}+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}^{(t)}_{e}-\hat{v}^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right]

(69)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\dfrac{5(L_{\nabla a}^{*})^{2}\omega_{B}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2}}\sum_{\iota=e}^{E-1}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T_{1}+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}_{\iota+1}^{(t)}-\hat{v}_{\iota+1}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]+\dfrac{5(L_{\nabla a}^{*})^{2}\omega_{B}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2}}\sum_{\iota=e}^{E-1}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{v}_{\iota+1}^{(t+1)}-\hat{v}_{\iota+1}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+(L_{\nabla a}^{*})^{2}(2(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2})\sum_{\iota=e}^{E-1}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T_{1}+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{\iota}^{(t)}-\hat{y}_{\iota}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]+\dfrac{20(L_{\nabla a}^{*})^{2}(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2}\omega_{B}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2}}\sum_{\iota=e}^{E-1}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|w_{\iota+1}^{(t+1)}-w_{\iota+1}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\dfrac{80(L_{\nabla a}^{*})^{2}(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2}\omega_{B}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2}(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}\sum_{\iota=e}^{E-1}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{y}}_{\iota}^{(t+1)}-{{y}}_{\iota}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]+\dfrac{80(L_{\nabla a}^{*})^{2}(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2}\omega_{B}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2}(1-\omega_{F})}\sum_{\iota=e}^{E-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{\iota}^{(T_{0})}-{{y}}_{\iota}^{(T_{0})}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\dfrac{5(L_{\nabla a}^{*})^{2}\omega_{B}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2}}\sum_{\iota=e}^{E-2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}_{\iota+1}^{(T_{0})}-\hat{v}_{\iota+1}^{(T_{0})}\right\|^{2}\right]+2(L_{\nabla a}^{*})^{2}\dfrac{\omega_{B}}{1-\omega_{B}}\sum_{\iota=e}^{E-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}^{(T_{0})}_{\iota}-{{v}}^{(T_{0})}_{\iota}\right\|^{2}\right]

holds for
e=1,2,\cdots,E-1
, where
(L_{\nabla a}^{*})^{2}:=\max\{1,(2(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2})^{E}\}\geq 0
.

For
e=1,2,\cdots,E-1
, define
\{C_{v,e}\}
as:

\displaystyle C_{v,1}=1,\quad C_{v,e}=1+\sum_{\iota=1}^{e-1}C_{v,\iota}\dfrac{5(L_{\nabla a}^{*})^{2}{\omega_{B}^{2}}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2}}\quad(e=2,\cdots,E-1).

(70)

Then taking summation on both sides of (
69
) over
e=1,2,\cdots,E-1
, we have:

\displaystyle\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}C_{v,e}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T_{1}+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}^{(t)}_{e}-\hat{v}^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right]\leq\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}\left(\sum_{\iota=1}^{e-1}C_{v,\iota}\dfrac{5(L_{\nabla a}^{*})^{2}\omega_{B}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2}}\right)\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T_{1}+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}_{e}^{(t)}-\hat{v}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

(71)

\displaystyle+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}\left(\sum_{\iota=1}^{e-1}C_{v,\iota}\dfrac{5(L_{\nabla a}^{*})^{2}\omega_{B}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2}}\right)\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{v}_{e}^{(t+1)}-\hat{v}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}\left(\sum_{\iota=1}^{e}C_{v,\iota}(L_{\nabla a}^{*})^{2}(2(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2})\right)\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T_{1}+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t)}-\hat{y}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{e=1}^{E}\left(\sum_{\iota=1}^{e-1}C_{v,\iota}\dfrac{20(L_{\nabla a}^{*})^{2}(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2}\omega_{B}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2}}\right)\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|w_{e}^{(t+1)}-w_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}\left(\sum_{\iota=1}^{e}C_{v,\iota}\dfrac{80(L_{\nabla a}^{*})^{2}(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2}\omega_{B}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2}(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}\right)\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{y}}_{e}^{(t+1)}-{{y}}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\hskip-1.42262pt\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}\left(\sum_{\iota=1}^{e-1}C_{v,\iota}\dfrac{5(L_{\nabla a}^{*})^{2}\omega_{B}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2}}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}_{e}^{(T_{0})}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt\hat{v}_{e}^{(T_{0})}\right\|^{2}\right]\hskip-1.42262pt+\hskip-1.42262pt\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}\left(\sum_{\iota=1}^{e}C_{v,\iota}2(L_{\nabla a}^{*})^{2}\dfrac{\omega_{B}}{1-\omega_{B}}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}^{(T_{0})}_{e}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt{{v}}^{(T_{0})}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}\left(\sum_{\iota=1}^{e}C_{v,\iota}\dfrac{80(L_{\nabla a}^{*})^{2}(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2}\omega_{B}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2}(1-\omega_{F})}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(T_{0})}-{{y}}_{e}^{(T_{0})}\right\|^{2}\right].

Then, with the definition of
C_{v,e}
in Eq. (
70
), we have:

\displaystyle\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T_{1}+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}^{(t)}_{e}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt\hat{v}^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}C_{y,e}^{\circ}\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T_{1}+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t)}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt\hat{y}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]+\sum_{e=1}^{E}C_{W,e}^{\circ}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|w_{e}^{(t+1)}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262ptw_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}C_{v,e}^{\circ}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{v}_{e}^{(t+1)}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt\hat{v}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}C_{\theta,e}^{\circ}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{y}}_{e}^{(t+1)}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt{{y}}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}C_{\theta,e}^{1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(T_{0})}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt{{y}}_{e}^{(T_{0})}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}C_{v,e}^{1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}_{e}^{(T_{0})}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt\hat{v}_{e}^{(T_{0})}\right\|^{2}\right]+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}C_{v,e}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}_{e}^{(T_{0})}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt{{v}}_{e}^{(T_{0})}\right\|^{2}\right],

where the coefficients are defined as follows:

\displaystyle C_{v,e}^{\circ}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\iota=1}^{e-1}C_{v,\iota}\dfrac{5(L_{\nabla a}^{*})^{2}{\omega_{B}^{2}}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2}}\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\dfrac{{\omega_{B}^{2}}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2(e-1)}}\right),

(72a)

\displaystyle C_{v,e}^{1}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\iota=1}^{e-1}C_{v,\iota}\dfrac{5(L_{\nabla a}^{*})^{2}{\omega_{B}^{2}}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2}}\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\dfrac{{\omega_{B}^{2}}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2(e-1)}}\right),

(72b)

\displaystyle C_{v,e}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\iota=1}^{e}C_{v,\iota}\dfrac{2(L_{\nabla a}^{*})^{2}{\omega_{B}}}{1-\omega_{B}}\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\dfrac{{\omega_{B}}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2e-1}}\right),

(72c)

\displaystyle C_{W,e}^{\circ}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\iota=1}^{e-1}C_{v,\iota}\dfrac{20(L_{\nabla a}^{*})^{2}(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2}\omega_{B}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2}}\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\dfrac{{\omega_{B}^{2}}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2(e-1)}}\right),

(72d)

\displaystyle C_{y,e}^{\circ}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\iota=1}^{e}C_{v,\iota}(L_{\nabla a}^{*})^{2}(2(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2})\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\dfrac{{1}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2(e-1)}}\right),\quad\hskip 0.85358pt

(72e)

\displaystyle C_{\theta,e}^{\circ}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\iota=1}^{e}C_{v,\iota}\dfrac{80(L_{\nabla a}^{*})^{2}(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2}\omega_{B}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2}(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\dfrac{{\omega_{B}^{2}}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2e}(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}\right),

(72f)

\displaystyle C_{\theta,e}^{1}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\iota=1}^{e}C_{v,\iota}\dfrac{80(L_{\nabla a}^{*})^{2}(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2}\omega_{B}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2}(1-\omega_{F})}\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\dfrac{{\omega_{B}^{2}}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2e}(1-\omega_{F})}\right).

(72g)

∎

Now, we try to combine the result of Eq. (
68
) with Eq. (
13
). For
Clapping
-FU , it holds for any
r=1,2,\cdots,r_{0}
that:

\displaystyle\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}\sum_{t=Q_{r}+1}^{Q_{r+1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}^{(t)}_{e}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt\hat{v}^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right]=\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}\sum_{t=Q_{r}+1}^{Q_{r+1}-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}^{(t)}_{e}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt\hat{v}^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right]

(73)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}C_{y,e}^{\circ}\sum_{t=Q_{r}+1}^{Q_{r+1}-1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t)}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt\hat{y}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]+\sum_{e=1}^{E}C_{W,e}^{\circ}\sum_{t=Q_{r}}^{Q_{r+1}-2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|w_{e}^{(t+1)}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262ptw_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}C_{v,e}^{\circ}\sum_{t=Q_{r}}^{Q_{r+1}-2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{v}_{e}^{(t+1)}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt\hat{v}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}C_{\theta,e}^{\circ}\sum_{t=Q_{r}}^{Q_{r+1}-2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{y}}_{e}^{(t+1)}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt{{y}}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right],

where it holds due to the fact that in
Clapping
-FU at
Q_{r}
-iteration it satisfies that

\displaystyle\tilde{v}_{e}^{(Q_{r})}={v}_{e}^{(Q_{r})}=\hat{v}_{e}^{(Q_{r})},\quad\tilde{y}_{e}^{(Q_{r})}={y}_{e}^{(Q_{r})}=\hat{y}_{e}^{(Q_{r})}.

Taking summation over
r
, it holds that:

\displaystyle\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}^{(t)}_{e}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt\hat{v}^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right]

(74)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}C_{y,e}^{\circ}\sum_{t=2}^{T+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t)}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt\hat{y}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]+\sum_{e=1}^{E}C_{W,e}^{\circ}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|w_{e}^{(t+1)}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262ptw_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}C_{v,e}^{\circ}\sum_{r=1}^{r_{0}}\sum_{t=Q_{r}}^{Q_{r+1}-2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{v}_{e}^{(t+1)}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt\hat{v}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}C_{\theta,e}^{\circ}\sum_{r=1}^{r_{0}}\sum_{t=Q_{r}}^{Q_{r+1}-2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{y}}_{e}^{(t+1)}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt{{y}}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right].

Plugging Eq. (
74
) into (
13
), we can find that:

\displaystyle\sum_{e=1}^{E}\sum_{t=1}^{T+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{u}^{(t)}_{e}-\nabla_{e}\ell(\boldsymbol{w}^{(t)})\right\|^{2}\right]

(75)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{e=1}^{E}\left(32L_{\nabla\ell}^{2}\left(\dfrac{p+m}{m^{2}(1-(1-p)(1-\frac{m}{2}))}+\dfrac{1}{m^{2}}\right)+8(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}C_{W,e}^{\circ}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|w^{(t+1)}_{e}-w^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}\left(8(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2}+8(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}C_{y,e}^{\circ}\right)\sum_{t=2}^{T+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t)}-\hat{y}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}8(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}C_{v,e}^{\circ}\sum_{r=1}^{r_{0}}\sum_{t=Q_{r}}^{Q_{r+1}-2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{v}_{e}^{(t+1)}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt\hat{v}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}8(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}C_{\theta,e}^{\circ}\sum_{r=1}^{r_{0}}\sum_{t=Q_{r}}^{Q_{r+1}-2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{y}}_{e}^{(t+1)}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt{{y}}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+4T\sigma^{2}\dfrac{(2-p)m-(1-p)m^{2}}{1-(1-p)(1-m)^{2}}+\dfrac{3}{m}\sum_{e=1}^{E}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{u}^{(1)}_{e}-\nabla_{e}\ell(\boldsymbol{w}^{(1)})\right\|^{2}\right].

Moreover for
Clapping
-FC , it holds from Eq. (
68
) that:

\displaystyle\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}\sum_{t=2}^{T+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}^{(t)}_{e}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt\hat{v}^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right]

(76)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}C_{y,e}^{\circ}\sum_{t=2}^{T+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t)}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt\hat{y}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]+\sum_{e=1}^{E}C_{W,e}^{\circ}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|w_{e}^{(t+1)}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262ptw_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}C_{v,e}^{\circ}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{v}_{e}^{(t+1)}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt\hat{v}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}C_{\theta,e}^{\circ}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{y}}_{e}^{(t+1)}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt{{y}}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}C_{\theta,e}^{1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(1)}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt{{y}}_{e}^{(1)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}C_{v,e}^{1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}_{e}^{(1)}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt\hat{v}_{e}^{(1)}\right\|^{2}\right]+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}C_{v,e}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}_{e}^{(1)}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt{{v}}_{e}^{(1)}\right\|^{2}\right].

Plugging Eq. (
76
) into (
13
), we can find that:

\displaystyle\sum_{e=1}^{E}\sum_{t=2}^{T+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{u}^{(t)}_{e}-\nabla_{e}\ell(\boldsymbol{w}^{(t)})\right\|^{2}\right]

(77)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{e=1}^{E}\left(32L_{\nabla\ell}^{2}\left(\dfrac{p+m}{m^{2}(1-(1-p)(1-\frac{m}{2}))}+\dfrac{1}{m^{2}}\right)+8(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}C_{W,e}^{\circ}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|w^{(t+1)}_{e}-w^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}\left(8(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2}+8(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}C_{y,e}^{\circ}\right)\sum_{t=2}^{T+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t)}-\hat{y}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}8(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}C_{v,e}^{\circ}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{v}_{e}^{(t+1)}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt\hat{v}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}8(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}C_{\theta,e}^{\circ}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{y}}_{e}^{(t+1)}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt{{y}}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+4T\sigma^{2}\dfrac{(2\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262ptp)m\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt(1\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262ptp)m^{2}}{1\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt(1\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262ptp)(1\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262ptm)^{2}}\hskip-1.42262pt+\hskip-1.42262pt\dfrac{3}{m}\sum_{e=1}^{E}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{u}^{(1)}_{e}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt\nabla_{e}\ell(\boldsymbol{w}^{(1)})\right\|^{2}\right]+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}8(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}C_{\theta,e}^{1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(1)}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt{{y}}_{e}^{(1)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}8(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}C_{v,e}^{1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}_{e}^{(1)}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt\hat{v}_{e}^{(1)}\right\|^{2}\right]+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}8(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}C_{v,e}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}_{e}^{(1)}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt{{v}}_{e}^{(1)}\right\|^{2}\right].

Both Eq. (
75
) and Eq. (
77
) calls for the further analysis of the term:

\displaystyle\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}\left(8(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2}+8(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}C_{y,e}^{\circ}\right)\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T_{1}+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t)}-\hat{y}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}8(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}C_{\theta,e}^{\circ}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{y}}_{e}^{(t+1)}-{{y}}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+

\displaystyle\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}8(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}C_{v,e}^{\circ}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{v}_{e}^{(t+1)}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt\hat{v}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

for any
T_{1}>T_{0}\geq 1
. Thus, we present the analysis as the following lemma:

Lemma 11
.

Suppose Assumption
1
and
3
holds, then there exist coefficients
C_{y,\theta,e}^{\circ}
and
C_{y,\theta,e}^{1}
defined by (
85
) for each
e=1,2,\cdots,E-1
and
T_{1}>T_{0}\geq 1
such that:

\displaystyle\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}\left(8(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2}+8(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}C_{y,e}^{\circ}\right)\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T_{1}+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t)}-\hat{y}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}8(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}C_{\theta,e}^{\circ}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{y}}_{e}^{(t+1)}-{{y}}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

(78)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}L_{a}^{2}\left(\sum_{\iota=e}^{E-1}C_{y,\theta,\iota}^{\circ}\left(\dfrac{8L_{a}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}\right)^{\iota-e}\right)\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|w_{e}^{(t+1)}-w_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\left(\sum_{\iota=1}^{E-1}C_{y,\theta,\iota}^{\circ}L_{a}^{2}\left(\dfrac{8L_{a}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}\right)^{\iota-1}\right)\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x^{(t+1)}-x^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}\left(C_{y,\theta,e}^{1}+\sum_{\iota=e+1}^{E-1}\dfrac{8L_{a}^{2}}{1-\omega_{F}}\left(\dfrac{8L_{a}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}\right)^{\iota-e-1}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}^{(T_{0})}_{e}-{{y}}^{(T_{0})}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right].

Proof.

For
e=2,\cdots,E-1
and
t=T_{0},\cdots,T_{1}
, we have:

\displaystyle\left\|{{y}}_{e}^{(t+1)}-{{y}}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}=\left\|a_{e}(\tilde{{{y}}}_{e-1}^{(t+1)},w_{e}^{(t+1)})-a_{e}(\tilde{{{y}}}_{e-1}^{(t)},w_{e}^{(t)})\right\|^{2}

(79)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle L_{a}^{2}\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{e-1}^{(t+1)}-\tilde{{{y}}}_{e-1}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}+L_{a}^{2}\left\|w_{e}^{(t+1)}-w_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}.

Taking expectation and then taking summation on boths over
t
, we can get:

\displaystyle\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{y}}_{e}^{(t+1)}-{{y}}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]\leq L_{a}^{2}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{e-1}^{(t+1)}-\tilde{{{y}}}_{e-1}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]+L_{a}^{2}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|w_{e}^{(t+1)}-w_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

(80)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\dfrac{8L_{a}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{y}}_{e-1}^{(t+1)}-{{y}}_{e-1}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]+\dfrac{8L_{a}^{2}}{1-\omega_{F}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{e-1}^{(T_{0})}-{{y}}_{e-1}^{(T_{0})}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+L_{a}^{2}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|w_{e}^{(t+1)}-w_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right],

where the first inequality is due to Assumption
1
and the second inequality is due to (
45
).

Then, we have:

\displaystyle

\displaystyle\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{y}}_{e}^{(t+1)}-{{y}}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

(81)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\dfrac{8L_{a}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}\right)^{e-1}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{y}}_{1}^{(t+1)}-{{y}}_{1}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]+\sum_{\iota=1}^{e-1}\dfrac{8L_{a}^{2}}{1-\omega_{F}}\left(\dfrac{8L_{a}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}\right)^{e-1-\iota}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{\iota}^{(T_{0})}-{{y}}_{\iota}^{(T_{0})}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{\iota=2}^{e}L_{a}^{2}\left(\dfrac{8L_{a}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}\right)^{e-\iota}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|w_{\iota}^{(t+1)}-w_{\iota}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right].

Moreover, from Assumption
1
we get:

\displaystyle

\displaystyle\left\|{{y}}_{1}^{(t+1)}-{{y}}_{1}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}=\left\|a_{1}(x^{(t+1)},w_{1}^{(t+1)})-a_{1}(x^{(t)},w_{1}^{(t)})\right\|^{2}

(82)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle L_{a}^{2}\left\|x^{(t+1)}-x^{(t)}\right\|^{2}+L_{a}^{2}\left\|w_{1}^{(t+1)}-w_{1}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}.

Plugging (
82
) into (
81
), then we have:

\displaystyle

\displaystyle\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{y}}_{e}^{(t+1)}-{{y}}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

(83)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{\iota=1}^{e}L_{a}^{2}\left(\dfrac{8L_{a}^{2}}{(1\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt\omega_{F})^{2}}\right)^{e-\iota}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|w_{\iota}^{(t+1)}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262ptw_{\iota}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]\hskip-1.42262pt+\hskip-1.42262ptL_{a}^{2}\left(\dfrac{8L_{a}^{2}}{(1\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt\omega_{F})^{2}}\right)^{e-1}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x^{(t+1)}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262ptx^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{\iota=1}^{e-1}\dfrac{8L_{a}^{2}}{1\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt\omega_{F}}\left(\dfrac{8L_{a}^{2}}{(1\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt\omega_{F})^{2}}\right)^{e-1-\iota}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{\iota}^{(T_{0})}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt{{y}}_{\iota}^{(T_{0})}\right\|^{2}\right].

Then, consider Eq. (
44
), we have:

\displaystyle\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}\left(8(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2}+8(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}C_{y,e}^{\circ}\right)\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T_{1}+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t)}-\hat{y}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}8(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}C_{\theta,e}^{\circ}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{y}}_{e}^{(t+1)}-{{y}}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

(84)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}\left(8(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2}+8(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}C_{y,e}^{\circ}\right)\sum_{\iota=1}^{e}2(2L_{a}^{2})^{e-\iota}\dfrac{\omega_{F}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{y}}^{(t+1)}_{\iota}-{{y}}^{(t)}_{\iota}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}\left(8(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2}+8(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}C_{y,e}^{\circ}\right)\sum_{\iota=1}^{e}2(2L_{a}^{2})^{e-\iota}\dfrac{\omega_{F}}{1-\omega_{F}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}^{(T_{0})}_{\iota}-{{y}}^{(T_{0})}_{\iota}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}8(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}C_{\theta,e}^{\circ}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{y}}_{e}^{(t+1)}-{{y}}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}C_{y,\theta,e}^{\circ}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{y}}_{e}^{(t+1)}-{{y}}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}C_{y,\theta,e}^{1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}^{(T_{0})}_{e}-{{y}}^{(T_{0})}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right],

where

\displaystyle C_{y,\theta,e}^{\circ}

\displaystyle=8(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}C_{\theta,e}^{\circ}+\dfrac{2\omega_{F}^{2}\sum_{\iota=e}^{E-1}\left(8(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2}+8(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}C_{y,\iota}^{\circ}\right)(2L_{a}^{2})^{\iota-e}}{(1-\omega_{F})^{2}},

(85a)

\displaystyle C_{y,\theta,e}^{1}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{2\omega_{F}\sum_{\iota=e}^{E-1}\left(8(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2}+8(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}C_{y,\iota}^{\circ}\right)(2L_{a}^{2})^{\iota-e}}{1-\omega_{F}}.

(85b)

From (
72
) we know that:

\displaystyle C_{y,e}^{\circ}

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\dfrac{{1}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2(e-1)}}\right),\quad C_{\theta,e}^{\circ}\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\dfrac{{\omega_{B}^{2}}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2e}(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}\right).

\displaystyle C_{y,\theta,e}^{\circ}

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\dfrac{{\omega_{B}^{2}}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2}(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}+\dfrac{{\omega_{F}^{2}}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2(E-2)}(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}\right),

\displaystyle C_{y,\theta,e}^{1}

\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\dfrac{{\omega_{F}^{2}}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2(E-2)}(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}\right).

Then, plugging (
83
) into (
84
), we can get:

\displaystyle\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}\left(8(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2}+8(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}C_{y,e}^{\circ}\right)\sum_{t=T_{0}+1}^{T_{1}+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t)}-\hat{y}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}8(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}C_{\theta,e}^{\circ}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{y}}_{e}^{(t+1)}-{{y}}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}L_{a}^{2}\left(\sum_{\iota=e}^{E-1}C_{y,\theta,\iota}^{\circ}\left(\dfrac{8L_{a}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}\right)^{\iota-e}\right)\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|w_{e}^{(t+1)}-w_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\left(\sum_{\iota=1}^{E-1}C_{y,\theta,\iota}^{\circ}L_{a}^{2}\left(\dfrac{8L_{a}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}\right)^{\iota-1}\right)\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x^{(t+1)}-x^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}\left(C_{y,\theta,e}^{1}+\sum_{\iota=e+1}^{E-1}\dfrac{8L_{a}^{2}}{1-\omega_{F}}\left(\dfrac{8L_{a}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}\right)^{\iota-e-1}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}^{(T_{0})}_{e}-{{y}}^{(T_{0})}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right].

∎

Finally, we consider the term
\left\|\hat{v}_{e}^{(t+1)}-\hat{v}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}
:

Lemma 12
.

Suppose Assumption
1
and
3
holds, then there exist coefficients
C_{v,x}^{\circ}
and
C_{v,w,e}^{\circ}
defined by (
92
) for each
e=1,2,\cdots,E
and
T_{1}>T_{0}\geq 1
such that:

\displaystyle\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}8(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}C_{v,e}^{\circ}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{v}_{e}^{(t+1)}-\hat{v}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

(86)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{v,x}^{\circ}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x^{(t+1)}-x^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]+\sum_{e=1}^{E}C_{v,w,e}^{\circ}\sum_{t=T_{0}}^{T_{1}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|w_{e}^{(t+1)}-w_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right].

Proof.

For every
e=1,2,\cdots,E-2
and
t=1,2,\cdots,T
and
T=T_{0},\cdots,T_{1}
, we have:

\displaystyle\left\|\hat{v}_{e}^{(t+1)}-\hat{v}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}=

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla_{1}a_{e+1}(\hat{y}_{e}^{(t+1)},W_{e+1}^{(t+1)})^{{}^{\mathsf{T}}}\hat{v}_{e+1}^{(t+1)}-\nabla_{1}a_{e+1}(\hat{y}_{e}^{(t)},W_{e+1}^{(t)})^{{}^{\mathsf{T}}}\hat{v}_{e+1}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}

(87)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2}\left(\left\|\hat{y}_{e}^{(t+1)}-\hat{y}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}+\left\|w_{e+1}^{(t+1)}-w_{e+1}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right)+(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}\left\|\hat{v}_{e+1}^{(t+1)}-\hat{v}_{e+1}^{(t)}\right\|^{2},

where the first inequality is due to (
12
).

Then we can get:

\displaystyle\left\|\hat{v}_{e}^{(t+1)}-\hat{v}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{\iota=e}^{E-1}(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2}((L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2})^{\iota-e}\left\|\hat{y}_{\iota}^{(t+1)}-\hat{y}_{\iota}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}

(88)

\displaystyle+\sum_{\iota=e+1}^{E}(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2}((L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2})^{\iota-e-1}\left\|w_{\iota}^{(t+1)}-w_{\iota}^{(t)}\right\|^{2},

where the second inequality is due to the definition of
\hat{v}_{E-1}^{(t)}
and Assumption
1
. And we can know that (
88
) also holds in the case of
e=E-1
.

Then we consider the term
\left\|\hat{y}_{e}^{(t+1)}-\hat{y}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}
. For
e=1,2,\cdots,E-1
, we obtain from Assumption
1
that:

\displaystyle\left\|\hat{y}_{e}^{(t+1)}-\hat{y}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}=

\displaystyle\left\|a_{e}(\hat{y}_{e-1}^{(t+1)},w_{e}^{(t+1)})-a_{e}(\hat{y}_{e-1}^{(t)},w_{e}^{(t)})\right\|^{2}

(89)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle L_{a}^{2}\left\|\hat{y}_{e-1}^{(t+1)}-\hat{y}_{e-1}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}+L_{a}^{2}\left\|{w}_{e}^{(t+1)}-{w}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}.

Then we have:

\displaystyle\left\|\hat{y}_{e}^{(t+1)}-\hat{y}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\leq\sum_{\iota=1}^{e}(L_{a}^{2})^{e-\iota+1}\left\|{w}_{\iota}^{(t+1)}-{w}_{\iota}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}+(L_{a}^{2})^{e}\left\|{x}^{(t+1)}-x^{(t)}\right\|^{2}.

(90)

Combining (
88
) and (
90
) together, then we can get:

\displaystyle\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}8(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}C_{v,e}^{\circ}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{v}_{e}^{(t+1)}-\hat{v}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

(91)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}\left(\sum_{\iota=1}^{e}8C_{v,\iota}^{\circ}(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2}((L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2})^{e-\iota+1}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{y}_{e}^{(t+1)}-\hat{y}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{e=2}^{E}\left(\sum_{\iota=1}^{e-1}8C_{v,\iota}^{\circ}(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2}((L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2})^{e-\iota}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|w_{e}^{(t+1)}-w_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}\left(\sum_{\iota=1}^{e}8C_{v,\iota}^{\circ}(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2}((L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2})^{e-\iota+1}\right)

\displaystyle\cdot\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{\eta=1}^{e}(L_{a}^{2})^{e-\eta+1}\left\|{w}_{\eta}^{(t+1)}-{w}_{\eta}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}+(L_{a}^{2})^{e}\left\|{x}^{(t+1)}-x^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{e=2}^{E}\left(\sum_{\iota=1}^{e-1}8C_{v,\iota}^{\circ}(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2}((L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2})^{e-\iota}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|w_{e}^{(t+1)}-w_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{v,x}^{\circ}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x^{(t+1)}-x^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]+\sum_{e=1}^{E}C_{v,w,e}^{\circ}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|w_{e}^{(t+1)}-w_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right],

where

\displaystyle C_{v,x}^{\circ}

\displaystyle=\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}\left(\sum_{\iota=1}^{e}8C_{v,\iota}^{\circ}(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2}((L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2})^{e-\iota+1}\right)(L_{a}^{2})^{e},

(92a)

\displaystyle C_{v,w,e}^{\circ}

\displaystyle=\sum_{\iota=1}^{e-1}8C_{v,\iota}^{\circ}(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2}((L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2})^{e-\iota}+\sum_{\eta=e}^{E-1}\left(\sum_{\iota=1}^{\eta}8C_{v,\iota}^{\circ}(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2}((L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2})^{\eta-\iota+1}\right)(L_{a}^{2})^{\eta-e+1}.

(92b)

And from (
72
) we know that:

\displaystyle C_{v,x}^{\circ}\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\dfrac{{\omega_{B}^{2}}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2(E-2)}}\right),\quad C_{v,w,e}^{\circ}\leq\mathcal{O}\left(\dfrac{{\omega_{B}^{2}}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2(E-2)}}\right).

∎

A.6

Convergence rate of general cases

A.6.1

Convergence rate of
Clapping
-FU

Based on (
8
), (
75
), (
78
), and (
86
), we can present the final convergence rate of
Clapping
-FU , which will be shown as the following lemma.

Lemma 13

(Convergence rate of
Clapping
-
FU
)
.

Suppose Assumption
1
-
4
hold. Then for Algorithm
4
there exist
\gamma,m,p>0
such that:

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla\ell(\boldsymbol{w}^{(t)})\right\|^{2}\right]\lesssim

\displaystyle\dfrac{\sigma}{\sqrt{T}}+\dfrac{1}{T(1-\omega_{B})^{E-1}(1-\omega_{F})^{E-1}}.

(93)

Proof.

Combining (
78
), and (
86
) together, it holds that:

\displaystyle\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}8(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}C_{v,e}^{\circ}\sum_{r=1}^{r_{0}}\sum_{t=Q_{r}}^{Q_{r+1}-2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\hat{v}_{e}^{(t+1)}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt\hat{v}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}8(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}C_{\theta,e}^{\circ}\sum_{r=1}^{r_{0}}\sum_{t=Q_{r}}^{Q_{r+1}-2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|{{y}}_{e}^{(t+1)}\hskip-1.42262pt-\hskip-1.42262pt{{y}}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

(94)

\displaystyle+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}\left(8(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2}+8(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}C_{y,e}^{\circ}\right)\sum_{t=2}^{T+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t)}-\hat{y}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{e=1}^{E}\left(L_{a}^{2}\left(\sum_{\iota=e}^{E-1}C_{y,\theta,\iota}^{\circ}\left(\dfrac{8L_{a}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}\right)^{\iota-e}\right)+C_{v,w,e}^{\circ}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|w_{e}^{(t+1)}-w_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right].

Eq. (
94
) holds is also due to the fact that
x^{(t+1)}=x^{(t)}
for any
t=Q_{r},\cdots,Q_{r+1}-2
,
{y}_{e}^{(Q_{r})}=\hat{y}_{e}^{(Q_{r})}=\tilde{y}_{e}^{(Q_{r})}
, and
{v}_{e}^{(Q_{r})}=\hat{v}_{e}^{(Q_{r})}=\tilde{v}_{e}^{(Q_{r})}
for any
r=1,2,\cdots,r_{0}
.

Combining (
75
), (
78
), and (
86
) together, we can obtain:

\displaystyle\sum_{e=1}^{E}\sum_{t=1}^{T+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{u}^{(t)}_{e}-\nabla_{e}\ell(\boldsymbol{w}^{(t)})\right\|^{2}\right]

(95)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{e=1}^{E}\left(32L_{\nabla\ell}^{2}\left(\dfrac{p+m}{m^{2}(1-(1-p)(1-\frac{m}{2}))}+\dfrac{1}{m^{2}}\right)+C_{w,e}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|w^{(t+1)}_{e}-w^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+4T\sigma^{2}\dfrac{(2-p)m-(1-p)m^{2}}{1-(1-p)(1-m)^{2}}+\dfrac{3}{m}\sum_{e=1}^{E}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{u}^{(1)}_{e}-\nabla_{e}\ell(\boldsymbol{w}^{(1)})\right\|^{2}\right],

where

\displaystyle C_{w,e}=

\displaystyle 8(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}C_{W,e}^{\circ}+L_{a}^{2}\left(\sum_{\iota=e}^{E-1}C_{y,\theta,\iota}^{\circ}\left(\dfrac{8L_{a}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}\right)^{\iota-e}\right)+C_{v,w,e}^{\circ}

(96)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2(E-1)}(1-\omega_{F})^{2(E-1)}}.

Let
p=p_{0}=\mathcal{O}(1)
as a constant with respect to
\sigma,T,\omega_{F},\omega_{B}
. Then let

\displaystyle m\sim

\displaystyle\left(\dfrac{1}{(1-\omega_{B})^{E-1}(1-\omega_{F})^{E-1}}+\sigma\sqrt{T}\right)^{-1},\ m\leq 1,

\displaystyle\gamma\sim

\displaystyle\left(\dfrac{1}{(1-\omega_{B})^{E-1}(1-\omega_{F})^{E-1}}+\sigma\sqrt{T}\right)^{-1},\ \gamma\leq 1.

At this time, if
\gamma/m
is sufficiently small, we can obtain that

32L_{\nabla\ell}^{2}\left(\dfrac{p+m}{m^{2}(1-(1-p)(1-\frac{m}{2}))}+\dfrac{1}{m^{2}}\right)+C_{w,e}-\dfrac{1}{2\gamma^{2}}\leq 0.

Then we have from Eq. (
8
):

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla\ell(\boldsymbol{w}^{(t)})\right\|^{2}\right]\lesssim

\displaystyle\dfrac{\sigma}{\sqrt{T}}+\dfrac{1}{T(1-\omega_{B})^{E-1}(1-\omega_{F})^{E-1}}.

∎

A.6.2

Convergence rate of
Clapping
-FC

Based on (
8
), (
77
), (
78
), and (
86
), we can present the final convergence lemma of the general cases, which will be shown as the following lemma.

Lemma 14

(Convergence rate of
Clapping
-
FC
)
.

Suppose Assumption
1
-
4
hold. Then for Algorithm
4
there exist
\gamma,m>0
such that:

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla\ell(\boldsymbol{w}^{(t)})\right\|^{2}\right]\lesssim\dfrac{\sigma^{\frac{4}{3}}}{T^{\frac{1}{3}}(1-\omega_{B})^{\frac{4(E-1)}{3}}(1-\omega_{F})^{\frac{4(E-1)}{3}}}

(97)

\displaystyle+\dfrac{1}{T}\left(\dfrac{1}{(1-\omega_{B})^{E-1}(1-\omega_{F})^{E-1}}+\dfrac{\omega_{F}^{2}+\omega_{B}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2(E-1)}(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}+\dfrac{1}{(1-\omega_{F})^{2(E-2)-1}}\right).

Proof.

Combining (
77
), (
78
), and (
86
) together, we can obtain:

\displaystyle\sum_{e=1}^{E}\sum_{t=2}^{T+1}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{u}^{(t)}_{e}-\nabla_{e}\ell(\boldsymbol{w}^{(t)})\right\|^{2}\right]

(98)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{e=1}^{E}\left(32L_{\nabla\ell}^{2}\left(\dfrac{p+m}{m^{2}(1-(1-p)(1-\frac{m}{2}))}+\dfrac{1}{m^{2}}\right)+C_{w,e}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|w^{(t+1)}_{e}-w^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+C_{x}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x^{(t+1)}-x^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}C_{y,\theta,e}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}^{(1)}_{e}-{{y}}^{(1)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right]+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}C_{v,e}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}_{e}^{(1)}-\hat{v}_{e}^{(1)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}C_{v,\chi,e}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}_{e}^{(1)}-{{v}}_{e}^{(1)}\right\|^{2}\right]+4T\sigma^{2}\dfrac{(2-p)m-(1-p)m^{2}}{1-(1-p)(1-m)^{2}}

\displaystyle+\dfrac{3}{m}\sum_{e=1}^{E}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{u}^{(1)}_{e}-\nabla_{e}\ell(\boldsymbol{w}^{(1)})\right\|^{2}\right],

where

\displaystyle C_{w,e}=

\displaystyle 8(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}C_{W,e}^{\circ}+L_{a}^{2}\left(\sum_{\iota=e}^{E-1}C_{y,\theta,\iota}^{\circ}\left(\dfrac{8L_{a}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}\right)^{\iota-e}\right)+C_{v,w,e}^{\circ}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2(E-1)}(1-\omega_{F})^{2(E-1)}},

(99a)

\displaystyle C_{x}=

\displaystyle C_{v,x}^{\circ}+\sum_{\iota=1}^{E-1}C_{y,\theta,\iota}^{\circ}L_{a}^{2}\left(\dfrac{8L_{a}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}\right)^{\iota-1}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\dfrac{\omega_{F}^{2}+\omega_{B}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2(E-1)}(1-\omega_{F})^{2(E-1)}},

(99b)

\displaystyle C_{y,\theta,e}=

\displaystyle 8(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}C_{\theta,e}^{1}+C_{y,\theta,e}^{1}+\sum_{\iota=e+1}^{E-1}\dfrac{8L_{a}^{2}}{1-\omega_{F}}\left(\dfrac{8L_{a}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}\right)^{\iota-e-1}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\dfrac{\omega_{B}^{2}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2e}(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}+\dfrac{{\omega_{F}^{2}}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2(E-2)}(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}+\dfrac{1}{(1-\omega_{F})^{2(E-e-2)+1}},

(99c)

\displaystyle C_{v,e}=

\displaystyle 8(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}C_{v,e}^{1},

(99d)

\displaystyle C_{v,\chi,e}=

\displaystyle 8(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2}C_{v,e}^{2}.

(99e)

Thus, we know that:

\displaystyle\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}C_{y,\theta,e}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}^{(1)}_{e}-{{y}}^{(1)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right]+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}C_{v,e}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}_{e}^{(1)}-\hat{v}_{e}^{(1)}\right\|^{2}\right]+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}C_{v,\chi,e}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}_{e}^{(1)}-{{v}}_{e}^{(1)}\right\|^{2}\right]

(100)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\dfrac{\omega_{B}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2(E-1)}(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}+\dfrac{{\omega_{F}^{2}}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2(E-2)}(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}+\dfrac{1}{(1-\omega_{F})^{2(E-2)-1}}.

Plugging (
95
) into (
8
), and use the fact that
\dfrac{p+m}{1-(1-p)(1-\frac{m}{2})}\leq 2
when
p,m\leq 1
, we can get:

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla\ell(\boldsymbol{w}^{(t)})\right\|^{2}\right]

(101)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\dfrac{2}{\gamma T}\mathbb{E}\left[\ell(\boldsymbol{w}^{(1)})-\inf_{\boldsymbol{w}}l(\boldsymbol{w})\right]+\dfrac{C_{x}}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|x^{(t+1)}-x^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\dfrac{1}{T}\sum_{e=1}^{E}\left(96L_{\nabla\ell}^{2}\dfrac{1}{m^{2}}+C_{w,e}-\dfrac{1}{2\gamma^{2}}\right)\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|w^{(t+1)}_{e}-w^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}\dfrac{C_{y,\theta,e}}{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}^{(1)}_{e}-{{y}}^{(1)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right]+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}\dfrac{C_{v,e}}{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}_{e}^{(1)}-\hat{v}_{e}^{(1)}\right\|^{2}\right]+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}\dfrac{C_{v,\chi,e}}{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}_{e}^{(1)}-{{v}}_{e}^{(1)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+4\sigma^{2}\dfrac{(2-p)m-(1-p)m^{2}}{1-(1-p)(1-m)^{2}}+\dfrac{3}{mT}\sum_{e=1}^{E}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{u}^{(1)}_{e}-\nabla_{e}\ell(\boldsymbol{w}^{(1)})\right\|^{2}\right]+\dfrac{1}{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla\ell(\boldsymbol{w}^{(1)})\right\|^{2}\right].

Taking
p=\sqrt{m}
where
h
is an undetermined function with respectively to
\omega_{F},\omega_{B}
, then the term of stochastic noise satisfies:

\displaystyle 4\sigma^{2}\dfrac{(2-p)m-(1-p)m^{2}}{1-(1-p)(1-m)^{2}}\leq 4\sigma^{2}\dfrac{(2-\sqrt{m})m-(1-\sqrt{m})m^{2}}{1-(1-\sqrt{m})(1-m)^{2}}\leq 4\sigma^{2}\cdot 2\sqrt{m}.

(102)

Let

\displaystyle m\sim

\displaystyle\left(\dfrac{1}{(1-\omega_{B})^{E-1}(1-\omega_{F})^{E-1}}+\dfrac{\sigma^{\frac{4}{3}}T^{\frac{2}{3}}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{\frac{4(E-1)}{3}}(1-\omega_{F})^{\frac{4(E-1)}{3}}}\right)^{-1},\ m\leq 1,

\displaystyle\gamma\sim

\displaystyle\left(\dfrac{1}{(1-\omega_{B})^{E-1}(1-\omega_{F})^{E-1}}+\dfrac{\sigma^{\frac{4}{3}}T^{\frac{2}{3}}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{\frac{4(E-1)}{3}}(1-\omega_{F})^{\frac{4(E-1)}{3}}}\right)^{-1},\ \gamma\leq 1.

At this time, if
\gamma/m
is sufficiently small, we can obtain that

96L_{\nabla\ell}^{2}\dfrac{1}{m^{2}}+C_{w,e}-\dfrac{1}{2\gamma^{2}}\leq 0.

Then with Assumption
4
, we have:

\displaystyle\dfrac{1}{T}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla l(W^{(t)})\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\dfrac{2}{\gamma T}\mathbb{E}\left[\ell(\boldsymbol{w}^{(1)})-\inf_{\boldsymbol{w}}l(\boldsymbol{w})\right]+C_{x}\sqrt{m}\varphi^{2}+8\sigma^{2}\sqrt{m}+\dfrac{3}{mT}\sum_{e=1}^{E}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{u}^{(1)}_{e}-\nabla_{e}\ell(\boldsymbol{w}^{(1)})\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}\dfrac{C_{y,\theta,e}}{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}^{(1)}_{e}-{{y}}^{(1)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\right]+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}\dfrac{C_{v,e}}{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}_{e}^{(1)}-\hat{v}_{e}^{(1)}\right\|^{2}\right]+\sum_{e=1}^{E-1}\dfrac{C_{v,\chi,e}}{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\tilde{v}_{e}^{(1)}-{{v}}_{e}^{(1)}\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\dfrac{1}{T}\mathbb{E}\left[\left\|\nabla\ell(\boldsymbol{w}^{(1)})\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\dfrac{\sigma^{\frac{4}{3}}}{T^{\frac{1}{3}}(1-\omega_{B})^{\frac{4(E-1)}{3}}(1-\omega_{F})^{\frac{4(E-1)}{3}}}

\displaystyle+\dfrac{1}{T}\left(\dfrac{1}{(1-\omega_{B})^{E-1}(1-\omega_{F})^{E-1}}+\dfrac{\omega_{F}^{2}+\omega_{B}}{(1-\omega_{B})^{2(E-1)}(1-\omega_{F})^{2}}+\dfrac{1}{(1-\omega_{F})^{2(E-2)-1}}\right).

∎

Remark 2
.

It can be observed that the conclusion in Lemma
1
can be directly obtained from Eq. (
101
).

Appendix B

Error propagation analysis

The following lemma states how the compressed error propagates during the forward and backward processes.

Lemma 15
.

Suppose Assumptions
1
and
3
hold. Then, for
e=1,\dots,E-1
, the error of the forward activation in Algorithm
4
can be bounded above as follows:

\displaystyle\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{e}^{(t)}-\hat{y}_{e}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}\leq\sum_{\iota=1}^{e}2(2L_{a}^{2})^{e-\iota}\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{\iota}^{(t)}-{{y}}_{\iota}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}.

(103)

For the error of backward gradients in Algorithm
4
, there exist constants
L_{\nabla a}^{\circ},L_{\nabla a}^{\prime}>0
such that:

\displaystyle\left\|\tilde{v}^{(t)}_{e}-\hat{v}^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}\leq

\displaystyle 2\sum_{\iota=e}^{E-1}(2(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2})^{\iota-e}\left\|\tilde{v}^{(t)}_{\iota}-{{v}}^{(t)}_{\iota}\right\|^{2}

(104)

\displaystyle+4(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2}\sum_{\iota=1}^{E-1}\sum_{s=\max\{e,\iota\}}^{E-1}(2(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2})^{s-e}(2L_{a}^{2})^{s-\iota}\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{\iota}^{(t)}-y_{\iota}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}.

Proof.

Eq. (
103
) is actually the same as Eq. (
43
). Then from (
63
) we can get:

\displaystyle\left\|\tilde{v}^{(t)}_{e}-\hat{v}^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}

(105)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\sum_{\iota=e}^{E-1}(2(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2})^{\iota-e}\left\|\tilde{v}^{(t)}_{\iota}-{{v}}^{(t)}_{\iota}\right\|^{2}+(2(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2})\sum_{\iota=e}^{E-1}(2(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2})^{\iota-e}\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{\iota}^{(t)}-\hat{y}_{\iota}^{(t)}\right\|^{2},

where the last inequality uses the fact that
L_{\nabla a}^{\prime}\geq L_{\nabla a}
in Remark
1
.

Then, plugging (
103
) into (
105
), we can obtain:

\displaystyle\left\|\tilde{v}^{(t)}_{e}-\hat{v}^{(t)}_{e}\right\|^{2}

(106)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\sum_{\iota=e}^{E-1}(2(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2})^{\iota-e}\left\|\tilde{v}^{(t)}_{\iota}-{{v}}^{(t)}_{\iota}\right\|^{2}+(2(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2})\sum_{\iota=e}^{E-1}(2(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2})^{\iota-e}\sum_{s=1}^{\iota}2(2L_{a}^{2})^{\iota-s}\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{s}^{(t)}-{{y}}_{s}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\sum_{\iota=e}^{E-1}(2(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2})^{\iota-e}\left\|\tilde{v}^{(t)}_{\iota}-{{v}}^{(t)}_{\iota}\right\|^{2}+4(L_{\nabla a}^{\prime})^{2}\sum_{\iota=1}^{E-1}\sum_{s=\max\{e,\iota\}}^{E-1}(2(L_{\nabla a}^{\circ})^{2})^{s-e}(2L_{a}^{2})^{s-\iota}\left\|\tilde{{{y}}}_{\iota}^{(t)}-y_{\iota}^{(t)}\right\|^{2}.

Then we know that Eq. (
104
) holds for
e=1,\cdots,E-1
.
∎

As indicated by Eq. (
103
), the error in forward activation is directly accumulated due to the compression operations of the preceding machines. Meanwhile, according to Eq. (
104
), the error in backward gradient evaluation, denoted as
\tilde{v}_{e}^{(t)}=\tilde{v}_{e+1}^{(t)}\nabla_{1}a_{e+1}(\tilde{y}_{e}^{(t)},W_{e}^{(t)})
, arises from two aspects. One aspect pertains to the accumulated errors during the backward compression in preceding machines, while the other relates to the error of the forward activation
\tilde{y}_{e}^{(t)}
. Moreover, the mathematical formulations presented in Eq. (
103
) and Eq. (
104
) jointly demonstrate that compression errors exhibit exponential amplification across distributed computing nodes. Furthermore, this analysis reveals a positive correlation between error magnitude and system complexity: as model dimensionality and parallelism scale increase, communication-induced compression errors emerge as a critical bottleneck in distributed training architectures.

Appendix C

Clapping
with Adam optimizer

In this section, we present
Clapping
equipped with Adam
[
25
]
optimizer, which can especially fit the need of pre-training and fine-tuning tasks for LLMs.

C.1

Algorithm design

Similar to the standard back-propagation algorithms with Adam optimizer, we introuduce two coefficients
\beta_{1},\beta_{2}\in(0,1)
and use
\nu_{e}^{(t)}
and
\upsilon_{e}^{(t)}
to record the first- and second-order optimizer states for the parameter
w_{e}^{(t)}
for
e=1,2,\cdots,E
, respectively. Then we use the following update rules to optimize
w_{e}^{(t)}
:

\displaystyle\tilde{u}_{e}^{(t)}

\displaystyle=(1-\beta_{1})\tilde{u}_{e}^{(t-1)}+\beta_{1}\nabla_{2}a_{e}(\tilde{y}^{(t)}_{e-1},w^{(t)}_{e})^{\mathsf{T}}\tilde{v}^{(t)}_{e},

(107a)

\displaystyle\upsilon_{e}^{(t)}

\displaystyle=(1-\beta_{2})\upsilon_{e}^{(t-1)}+\beta_{2}\left(\nabla_{2}a_{e}(\tilde{y}^{(t)}_{e-1},w^{(t)}_{e})^{\mathsf{T}}\tilde{v}^{(t)}_{e}\right)^{\odot 2},

(107b)

\displaystyle\nu^{(t)}_{e}

\displaystyle=\dfrac{\tilde{u}_{e}^{(t)}}{\sqrt{\upsilon_{e}^{(t)}+\varepsilon}},

(107c)

\displaystyle w_{e}^{(t+1)}

\displaystyle=w_{e}^{(t)}-\gamma\nu^{(t)}_{e},

(107d)

where
\odot 2
denotes the second moment and
\varepsilon>0
is a fixed constant. The division and addition operate in (
107c
) are all sample-wised. Then we can present
Clapping
with Adam optimizer as Algorithm
5
.

Algorithm 5

Clapping
with Adam optimizer

0:

Initialize
\tilde{y}^{(0)}_{e}=0,\tilde{v}^{(0)}_{e}=0,\upsilon^{(0)}_{e}=0,\tilde{u}^{(0)}_{e}=0
for
e=1,\cdots,E-1
. Initialize dataset
\mathcal{D}
, learning rate
\gamma_{t}
, compressor
\mathcal{C}
, and lazy sampling rate
\{p_{t}\}_{t=1}^{T}
.

for
t=1,\cdots,T
do

{x}^{(t)},f^{(t)}_{\text{FU}}={\mbox{{LazySampling}}(\mathcal{D},t,p_{t})}
, initialize
\tilde{y}^{(t)}_{0}={x}^{(t)}
, and let
\tilde{v}_{E}^{(t)}=1.

for
e=1,2,\cdots,E-1
do

{\mbox{Forward}_{e}(\tilde{{y}}_{e}^{(t-1)},\tilde{y}_{e-1}^{(t)},w_{e}^{(t)},f^{(t)}_{\text{FU}})}
,

end for

for
e=E,E-1,\cdots,1
do

Update
\upsilon^{(0)}_{e}
,
\tilde{u}^{(0)}_{e}
and
w_{e}^{(t+1)}
by (
107
), and take
